# M02 - Product implementation and repair tranche

## Module contract and selection

| Module type | Decision | Instance IDs | Reason |
|---|---|---|---|
| M02 | SELECTED | MI-NORMAL-001-BUILD, MI-FL2-S1-001-PATCH, MI-NORMAL-002-BUILD, MI-FL2-S1-002-PATCH, MI-NORMAL-003-BUILD, MI-FL2-S1-003-PATCH, MI-NORMAL-004-BUILD, MI-FL2-S1-004-PATCH, MI-NORMAL-005-BUILD, MI-FL2-S1-005-PATCH, MI-NORMAL-006-BUILD, MI-FL2-S1-006-PATCH, MI-NORMAL-007-BUILD, MI-FL2-S1-007-PATCH, MI-NORMAL-008-BUILD, MI-FL2-S1-008-PATCH, MI-NORMAL-009-BUILD, MI-FL2-S1-009-PATCH, MI-NORMAL-010-BUILD, MI-FL2-S1-010-PATCH | SELECTED: Give one serial writer the full coherent deliverable, its high-value failure brief, implementation, self-testing, and all later material repairs for the same unaccepted task. This product's exact identities, trust, real native/service proof and recovery require the configured occurrences; no extra runner or release ceremony. |

Give one serial writer the full coherent deliverable, its high-value failure brief, implementation,
self-testing, and all later material repairs for the same unaccepted task.

## Public interface and compatibility boundary

accepted baseline + complete deliverable/repair objective -> one reviewable scoped tip and self-check results. Concrete values and return targets belong to the owning step's configured instance. Private changes do not alter public semantics; update actual consumers for a real contract change.

## Rules, process, recipe actions, and allowed variations

P01–P15 own shared rules. Semantic choices in recipe verbs remain ROOT decisions; test_root only authors declared lane-local contracts. Workers mechanically execute supplied choices and return any missing meaning. Reviewer findings remain open within assigned boundaries. Each governing card cites all actions; member cards cite only their ordered subset. Parameters may select bounded source/test paths and optional profiles, never weaken acceptance or change authority.

| Order | Recipe action ID | Project-specific action/process | Allowed parameterization | Decision owner |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | M02-A1 | Verify starting source state, cleanliness, ownership, dependencies, and active claim/worktree IDs. | owning instance exact paths, proof goals, predecessor output and selected scope | root |
| 2 | M02-A2 | Read the initial entrypoints and governing requirement/acceptance rows; do not reconstruct history. | owning instance exact paths, proof goals, predecessor output and selected scope | root |
| 3 | M02-A3 | Convert the selected failure brief into implementation invariants and focused self-check targets. | owning instance exact paths, proof goals, predecessor output and selected scope | root |
| 4 | M02-A4 | Implement the complete coherent deliverable serially; internal commits do not create review gates. | owning instance exact paths, proof goals, predecessor output and selected scope | root |
| 5 | M02-A5 | Run the shortest self-checks that can catch producer mistakes before publishing. | owning instance exact paths, proof goals, predecessor output and selected scope | root |
| 6 | M02-A6 | Inspect the final diff for scope, unintended files, debug artifacts, and requirement coverage. | owning instance exact paths, proof goals, predecessor output and selected scope | root |
| 7 | M02-A7 | Produce one reviewable tip and publish the diff/check/result information its consumers need. | owning instance exact paths, proof goals, predecessor output and selected scope | root |
| 8 | M02-A8 | On an M05 material return, continue this same logical task and workflow role; reuse the active invocation when available and still selected, otherwise use its structured handoff. Consume the entire pooled finding set, repair all admitted findings as one batch, rerun only implicated self-checks, and publish one new tip. | owning instance exact paths, proof goals, predecessor output and selected scope | root |

- Self-check failure remains inside M02 only when it proves or leaves genuinely undecidable a required
  product criterion; continue from the first failed action/check until corrected, unrecoverable, or stalled.
- A complete frozen tip proceeds to M03 when independent assets must be constructed, otherwise to the
  graph-selected check or acceptance consumer.
- Only M05 may admit external findings into the material return. Do not repair reviewer comments piecemeal.
- A strict test-only or administrative correction never enters M02.
- A Series 1 M02 occurrence is separate from the step's normal M02 occurrence and is available only
  under `incremental-verification.md` after the complete feasible pool yields one scoped compatible
  correction objective. It repairs only that objective, runs the compile-plus-motivating-test smoke,
  never invokes the normal broad affected campaign, and ends at ROOT for the Series 1 review,
  integration, and repaired-output exit. A Series 2 M02 occurrence exists only when remaining
  checkpoint results establish a new material correction objective at the current progress bound; it
  is never an automatic replay of the normal implementation path.

## Configured module instances

### MI-NORMAL-001-BUILD - M02: Freeze the builder and qualify the development boundary / NORMAL / BUILD

#### Purpose

Implement the complete coherent domain brief below, with one writer and shortest self-checks. Independent test author/reviewer own acceptance evidence. Return one reviewable tip; later repairs consume ROOT's full diagnosed objective and protected scope, not raw suggestions. Normal complete stage work and its independently selected evidence.

#### Coverage

DEL-001; REQ-001, REQ-027, REQ-028, REQ-029; STEP-001 NORMAL.

#### Selection basis

Required product implementation and repair tranche
 boundary; Normal complete stage work and its independently selected evidence.

#### Owner and roles

root owns bounded instance mechanics; root retains global decisions. Dispatch inventory: CARD-NORMAL-001-BUILD-EXEC=writer

#### Preconditions

MI-NORMAL-001-ADMIT.typed output satisfying M02 public input is verified; exact role resolution and scope authorization precede launch.

#### Inputs

Pre-product CONTROL-READY is owned here: development/test-tools/tests/test_fixture_controls.py uses stdlib unittest and fixture finite children to exercise ROOT's actual dispatch/handle/refill/checkpoint/owned-descendant cleanup boundary. M02 owns only any genuinely needed pure test helper, M03 owns these independent fixture assets, and STEP-001 M08 consumes them before native baseline. It does not depend on product pyproject or STEP-002.

MI-NORMAL-001-ADMIT.typed output satisfying M02 public input; REQ-001, REQ-027, REQ-028, REQ-029; current candidate and consumed state/checkpoints. Complete domain brief:

1. Record the root plan's new frozen source identity/bytes and product baseline; exclude
   `.git` pointers, private config and mutable output from the deployment.
   A1's source conflict is resolved; qualify installed bindings with the explicit
   adapter options owned by plan-workflow.md Section10 and the isolated role mapping. Account for the uncommitted product refresh
   before selecting a lane's committed base. Record the chosen test-middleweight and
   read back the user's supplied role/APC bindings when preparing their first native wave; never substitute the older named-model matrix.
2. Inspect any existing monitor, active epoch and lane ownership before switching
   ROOT bindings. Drain/shut down only the exact old instance; keep initialized
   EverOS data and evidence. Prepare `B` and its two-key native config, pointing
   to the actual product Git workspace. Verify deployed source/payload equality.
   Source refresh is not runtime migration: regenerate through native setup after
   the ownership-safe switch, bootstrap a fresh epoch, and never resume legacy
   records missing `provider.launch_config`. Keep the optional evaluator disabled.
3. Map writable stores and loaded entrypoints before launch. Prepare separate
   candidate ROOT/runtime/worktrees, service ports, provider homes where needed,
   and scoped control configuration under `development/`. Outer setup must not
   overwrite an inner instance or use the stale reference workspace path.
4. Run the native all-off baseline launch -> bounded synthetic edit/check ->
   result -> ROOT review/acceptance -> retire/cleanup. Verify actual headless
   file/tool action, not only login or a textual response. Record the outer
   delegation and inner candidate ownership chain where native coverage is claimed.
5. Qualify a narrow test-ROOT task assignment using existing frozen controls.
   Ordinary workers lack control authority; test ROOT receives only test-scoped
   configuration. Validate inherited product-memory credentials are absent from
   ordinary worker prompt/env/config/tools, even for all-off tests.



Stage-local product gate (owned acceptance slice; executed by the later gate, not a command for the BUILD writer):

Baseline only: frozen/deployed byte identity, exact launch-option rejection/persistence, real candidate-owned routine/middle/heavy work and independent closeout review, all-off zero optional calls/context, native receipts, ownership and cleanup satisfy REQ-027/028 and the baseline subset of REQ-001. Full composition, switches and provider-context obligations close in STEP-007; integrated closure is STEP-009. Missing installed bindings/control capacity hold their consumers, never authorize builder expansion.

Domain brief describes the deliverable and its later evidence. M02 implements only assigned production/demo/documentation surfaces; M03 authors independent assets, M04 checks, M08 qualifies and M09 alone performs admitted live attempts. Live-run verbs in this brief are not M02 dispatch authority.

#### Outputs and results

MI-NORMAL-001-BUILD.reviewable product tip and smoke; native result and unique member output paths; factual reports are not acceptance.

#### Concurrency and isolation

Source frozen during collection. Separate native WORKTREE/member IDs and result/cache/temp roots; one writer at shared seams. M04/M07 independent paths launch before waiting; Section10 shared budget, no added scheduler.

#### Resources and side effects

`development/dogfood/`, `development/test-tools/`, disposable
`development/test-runs/` and `development/evidence/`; deliberately staged candidate
configuration/test assets in `P` only. Reference source remains unchanged. Credentials remain protected configuration only. No live effects in M08; M09 exact allowed synthetic targets. Native resource IDs recorded/read back before cleanup; no broad deletion. Sessions unbounded, finite policy per P02.

#### Checks and acceptance

Return the assigned coherent reviewable production/demo/documentation tip, actual available smoke results and explicit unresolved facts. Independent M03/M04/M05 determine acceptance; self-report is not a product gate. Compile/import the changed source with its accepted environment and run the shortest already-available source-focused self-check. If independent M03 tests are not authored yet, report that fact; their later execution is not a prerequisite for returning this reviewable tip. Do not run downstream live campaigns or final safeguard. Stage product criteria remain the normal BUILD brief's explicit gate slice and root Section3; completion of this module is not automatic product acceptance.

#### Failure and exception routes

ROOT applies P07 owner routing from the complete pool: material product failure returns to the declared originating production M02 owner, strict test-only to that asset's M03 owner under P08, and operational/report fault stays with its factual producer under P09. The observing step does not gain write authority; P04 returns accepted repairs to the current progress bound, and P15 contains only observed live harm.

#### Prior results and change effects

P04 maps source/schema/config/runner/fixtures and actual external target state; keep historical results and unchanged PASS, rerun only earliest affected/unresolved set; no whole-plan diff invalidation.

#### Repeat, join, and terminal behavior

P02/P04/P07; complete pool, one join; ROOT decision precedes next module dispatch. No worker self-dispatch or unbounded replacements.

#### Cost and critical-path effect

0.5–2 engineering days for owning stage, not per instance. This M02 uses 1 terminal worker assignments only when active; local commands estimated5–120s, native scenario2–10min, service30–300s. Normal complete stage work and its independently selected evidence. Reuse eliminates only work with unchanged inputs, no measured savings claimed.

#### Outputs and results

MI-FL2-S1-001-PATCH.reviewable product tip and smoke; native result and unique member output paths; factual reports are not acceptance.

#### Concurrency and isolation

Source frozen during collection. Separate native WORKTREE/member IDs and result/cache/temp roots; one writer at shared seams. M04/M07 independent paths launch before waiting; Section10 shared budget, no added scheduler.

#### Resources and side effects

`development/dogfood/`, `development/test-tools/`, disposable
`development/test-runs/` and `development/evidence/`; deliberately staged candidate
configuration/test assets in `P` only. Reference source remains unchanged. Credentials remain protected configuration only. No live effects in M08; M09 exact allowed synthetic targets. Native resource IDs recorded/read back before cleanup; no broad deletion. Sessions unbounded, finite policy per P02.

#### Checks and acceptance

Return the assigned coherent reviewable production/demo/documentation tip, actual available smoke results and explicit unresolved facts. Independent M03/M04/M05 determine acceptance; self-report is not a product gate. Run changed-source compile and ROOT-named exact motivating test only, retaining its smoke receipt; no broad campaign. Stage product criteria remain the normal BUILD brief's explicit gate slice and root Section3; completion of this module is not automatic product acceptance.

#### Failure and exception routes

ROOT applies P07 owner routing from the complete pool: material product failure returns to the declared originating production M02 owner, strict test-only to that asset's M03 owner under P08, and operational/report fault stays with its factual producer under P09. The observing step does not gain write authority; P04 returns accepted repairs to the current progress bound, and P15 contains only observed live harm.

#### Prior results and change effects

P04 maps source/schema/config/runner/fixtures and actual external target state; keep historical results and unchanged PASS, rerun only earliest affected/unresolved set; no whole-plan diff invalidation.

#### Repeat, join, and terminal behavior

After acceptance and separate integration, exit only to ROOT's selected current progress bound through its Series 2 entry. P02/P04/P07; complete pool, one join; accepted integrated repaired output exits to current-bound Series 2 only. No worker self-dispatch or unbounded replacements.

#### Cost and critical-path effect

0.5–2 engineering days for owning stage, not per instance. This M02 uses 1 terminal worker assignments only when active; local commands estimated5–120s, native scenario2–10min, service30–300s. Outbound scoped repair only. Required smoke is changed-source compile plus the exact motivating tests from ROOT's diagnosed correction card. No broad deterministic campaign; independent review and separate accepted integration retained. Reuse eliminates only work with unchanged inputs, no measured savings claimed.

### MI-NORMAL-002-BUILD - M02: Establish product contracts, privacy and durable local evidence / NORMAL / BUILD

#### Purpose

Implement the complete coherent domain brief below, with one writer and shortest self-checks. Independent test author/reviewer own acceptance evidence. Return one reviewable tip; later repairs consume ROOT's full diagnosed objective and protected scope, not raw suggestions. Normal complete stage work and its independently selected evidence.

#### Coverage

DEL-002; REQ-002, REQ-017, REQ-018, REQ-019, REQ-021; STEP-002 NORMAL.

#### Selection basis

Required product implementation and repair tranche
 boundary; Normal complete stage work and its independently selected evidence.

#### Owner and roles

root owns bounded instance mechanics; root retains global decisions. Dispatch inventory: CARD-NORMAL-002-BUILD-EXEC=complex_writer

#### Preconditions

MI-NORMAL-002-ADMIT.typed output satisfying M02 public input is verified; exact role resolution and scope authorization precede launch.

#### Inputs

MI-NORMAL-002-ADMIT.typed output satisfying M02 public input; REQ-002, REQ-017, REQ-018, REQ-019, REQ-021; current candidate and consumed state/checkpoints. Complete domain brief:

1. Define one versioned schema owner for objective/current-plan, normalized
   candidates, templates, approval/recipient partitions, final handoff, outcomes,
   usages and external operations. Keep application/project/namespace/owner,
   task/objective/decision/invocation and procedure origin/revision distinct.
   Define deterministic canonicalization, absence/unrecognized/predicate semantics,
   integrity, compatibility and migrations before durable records exist.
2. Create the product package and explicit locked dependencies. Keep the inherited
   all-off lifecycle available without initializing optional providers. Define
   supported operation inputs/units/results and realistic error/recovery behavior;
   avoid a generic plugin framework or second launcher.
3. Add SQLite transaction/unique-key primitives, immutable evidence storage,
   dispatch/side-effect intents, canonical outcomes and separate late usage.
   Exercise actual reopen, crash boundary and concurrent-writer behavior.
4. Resolve each feature to requested/effective value plus reason and identity.
   Implement explicit future-only errors for learned enable/load/update/training
   requests without installing or loading learner machinery.
5. Implement the known-secret/confidentiality policy at local/remote and dispatch
   boundaries. Bind authorized recipients and approved-content revisions; retain
   protected raw evidence, never silently edit mandatory truth or approved meaning.
6. Define narrow adapter ports for EverOS, procedure store, template registry and
   native harness handoff. Test independent serialized fixtures, not just two
   consumers importing and agreeing on the same incorrect assumption.
7. Before broad use of new fixture/collection helpers, satisfy CONTROL-READY in
   plan-workflow.md Section10 using retained applicable evidence or the focused synthetic
   finite-command exercise. Prove early terminal handling, independent completion,
   capacity refill, complete status accounting and owned-child cleanup. The
   STEP-001 fixture owner does the same before earlier reuse if helpers are added
   there. Qualify native-specific changed cleanup only when local proof is
   insufficient; retain unchanged control credit instead of rerunning it per stage.



Stage-local product gate (owned acceptance slice; executed by the later gate, not a command for the BUILD writer):

Foundation only: versioned identity/authority/schema round trips and critical-version rejection, privacy primitive known-secret and fail-closed payload expectations, SQLite atomic/reopen/concurrent-writer contracts, all-off dependency isolation and selected helper CONTROL-READY pass. Credit REQ-017/018 only for these primitives, not actual workers or every later consumer; consumer/native privacy closure belongs to STEP-007 and STEP-009. Accepted examples and fixtures are versioned outputs. Actual field names are owned code, not a second specification.

Domain brief describes the deliverable and its later evidence. M02 implements only assigned production/demo/documentation surfaces; M03 authors independent assets, M04 checks, M08 qualifies and M09 alone performs admitted live attempts. Live-run verbs in this brief are not M02 dispatch authority.

### MI-NORMAL-003-BUILD - M02: Prove the thin end-to-end product slice / NORMAL / BUILD

#### Purpose

Implement the complete coherent domain brief below, with one writer and shortest self-checks. Independent test author/reviewer own acceptance evidence. Return one reviewable tip; later repairs consume ROOT's full diagnosed objective and protected scope, not raw suggestions. Normal complete stage work and its independently selected evidence.

#### Coverage

DEL-003; REQ-002, REQ-003, REQ-004, REQ-019, REQ-020, REQ-022, REQ-025, REQ-026; STEP-003 NORMAL.

#### Selection basis

Required product implementation and repair tranche
 boundary; Normal complete stage work and its independently selected evidence.

#### Owner and roles

root owns bounded instance mechanics; root retains global decisions. Dispatch inventory: CARD-NORMAL-003-BUILD-EXEC=complex_writer

#### Preconditions

MI-NORMAL-003-ADMIT.typed output satisfying M02 public input is verified; exact role resolution and scope authorization precede launch.

#### Inputs

MI-NORMAL-003-ADMIT.typed output satisfying M02 public input; REQ-002, REQ-003, REQ-004, REQ-019, REQ-020, REQ-022, REQ-025, REQ-026; current candidate and consumed state/checkpoints. Complete domain brief:

1. Implement exact objective load, preserve an accepted same-objective plan,
   prepare/finalize and dispatch binding, linked terminal review/acceptance, one
   local outcome and safe reconciliation. Validate the actual task card and
   resolved Git base as well as the envelope itself.
2. Ingest one reviewed success and one reviewed failure through source-conformant
   EverOS requests; retain exact local evidence immediately and receipt/case links.
3. Implement the first product Atlas schema/index bootstrap and one authorized
   procedure publication with receiver-verifiable approval/current-state evidence.
   Use actual scoped Vector Search, then authoritative eligibility reads; do not
   count a MongoDB write/import as search proof.
4. Complete direct fill, fresh fallback and one real near-match adaptation using
   explicit `apc_adaptation_binding`, including candidate-side provider/model/effort
   configuration. ROOT authorizes the drafting task; child output is proposed
   data and cannot accept the parent plan, recurse, or implement parent code.
5. Demonstrate native child launch/result/usage/cleanup and separate ROOT plan
   acceptance, actual worker execution and linked parent outcome. Map the product's
   explicit role/APC bindings onto inherited validated `provider.launch_config`;
   reuse rather than reimplement configurable launch. No model ranking or named APC default.
6. Expose the existing typed validator/adapter seams to independent M03 two-choice binding-contract fixtures; production remains configurable, without hardcoded model names or synthetic fixture tokens. M04 consumes those fixtures as scoped local checks alongside the integration tests.
7. Reopen local state after an interrupted harmless read and an ambiguous effect;
   retain uncertain operation evidence instead of blindly replaying ingestion or
   launch. Track usage begun with missing receipts as incomplete.



Stage-local product gate (owned acceptance slice; executed by the later gate, not a command for the BUILD writer):

Thin slice only: actual reviewed experience success/failure provenance, real local extraction, scoped queryable Atlas hit, typed validated context, direct fill without a child, one successful profile A child adaptation, incompatible/failure fresh fallback, linked accepted outcome/usage and owned cleanup on a pinned candidate. This establishes architecture, the explicit two-choice configuration-contract fixture and actual A execution in REQ-026. #### Outputs and results

MI-FL2-S1-003-PATCH.reviewable product tip and smoke; native result and unique member output paths; factual reports are not acceptance.

#### Concurrency and isolation

Source frozen during collection. Separate native WORKTREE/member IDs and result/cache/temp roots; one writer at shared seams. M04/M07 independent paths launch before waiting; Section10 shared budget, no added scheduler.

#### Resources and side effects

minimum implementations in `P/src/memory_harness/`, a minimal reviewed
template, `P/tests/integration/`, candidate `P/harness/orchestrator_harness/`
bootstrap/resume/config/provider seams, and assigned synthetic service resources.
These candidate changes never modify the frozen builder. Credentials remain protected configuration only. No live effects in M08; M09 exact allowed synthetic targets. Native resource IDs recorded/read back before cleanup; no broad deletion. Sessions unbounded, finite policy per P02.

#### Checks and acceptance

Return the assigned coherent reviewable production/demo/documentation tip, actual available smoke results and explicit unresolved facts. Independent M03/M04/M05 determine acceptance; self-report is not a product gate. Run changed-source compile and ROOT-named exact motivating test only, retaining its smoke receipt; no broad campaign. Stage product criteria remain the normal BUILD brief's explicit gate slice and root Section3; completion of this module is not automatic product acceptance.

#### Failure and exception routes

ROOT applies P07 owner routing from the complete pool: material product failure returns to the declared originating production M02 owner, strict test-only to that asset's M03 owner under P08, and operational/report fault stays with its factual producer under P09. The observing step does not gain write authority; P04 returns accepted repairs to the current progress bound, and P15 contains only observed live harm.

#### Prior results and change effects

P04 maps source/schema/config/runner/fixtures and actual external target state; keep historical results and unchanged PASS, rerun only earliest affected/unresolved set; no whole-plan diff invalidation.

#### Repeat, join, and terminal behavior

After acceptance and separate integration, exit only to ROOT's selected current progress bound through its Series 2 entry. P02/P04/P07; complete pool, one join; accepted integrated repaired output exits to current-bound Series 2 only. No worker self-dispatch or unbounded replacements.

#### Cost and critical-path effect

2–5 engineering days for owning stage, not per instance. This M02 uses 1 terminal worker assignments only when active; local commands estimated5–120s, native scenario2–10min, service30–300s. Outbound scoped repair only. Required smoke is changed-source compile plus the exact motivating tests from ROOT's diagnosed correction card. No broad deterministic campaign; independent review and separate accepted integration retained. Reuse eliminates only work with unchanged inputs, no measured savings claimed.

### MI-NORMAL-004-BUILD - M02: Complete experience and generated-procedure provenance / NORMAL / BUILD

#### Purpose

Implement the complete coherent domain brief below, with one writer and shortest self-checks. Independent test author/reviewer own acceptance evidence. Return one reviewable tip; later repairs consume ROOT's full diagnosed objective and protected scope, not raw suggestions. Normal complete stage work and its independently selected evidence.

#### Coverage

DEL-004; REQ-004, REQ-005; STEP-004 NORMAL.

#### Selection basis

Required product implementation and repair tranche
 boundary; Normal complete stage work and its independently selected evidence.

#### Owner and roles

root owns bounded instance mechanics; root retains global decisions. Dispatch inventory: CARD-NORMAL-004-BUILD-EXEC=writer

#### Preconditions

STEP-003.accepted integrated output is verified; exact role resolution and scope authorization precede launch.

#### Inputs

STEP-003.accepted integrated output; REQ-004, REQ-005; current candidate and consumed state/checkpoints. Complete domain brief:

1. Capture suitable reviewed successes/failures with disproved hypotheses,
   original evidence and environment. Avoid thin administrative case promotion.
   Preserve recent evidence across closure/restart until searchable receipt
   linkage is confirmed; mark unavailable/unlinked provenance honestly.
2. Verify app/project/owner/root scope consistently across add/flush/search/get.
   Establish safe replay or exact reconciliation of message buffering and flush;
   include lost response, concurrent retry, ordering and extraction side effects.
   Where source cannot prove replay safety, hold uncertain intent without losing
   the reviewed trajectory or blocking valid task acceptance.
3. Permit automatic case extraction and proposed skill generation. Resolve every
   claimed source case by exact identity to reviewed receipt/content evidence.
   A complete lineage still requires trusted approval of the exact revision.
4. Enforce generation-off before service model calls, separately from case capture
   and generated-skill-use controls. If per-request control is inadequate, use
   isolated service/root configuration; filtering results afterward fails.
5. Confirm pagination/enumeration behavior under updates, exact resolver semantics,
   future-only jobs, and extraction/embedding/native usage linkage. Generated scripts
   remain uninstalled and unexecuted.



Stage-local product gate (owned acceptance slice; executed by the later gate, not a command for the BUILD writer):

Experience assertions REQ-004/005: real local extraction and exact review/acceptance lineage, source-adapter contract and restart/replay joins, uncertain acknowledgement reconciliation, suppression controls, exclusion of unapproved generated skill and inclusion of approved compatible guidance. STEP-009 checks integrated consumption and reuse, not a duplicate role sweep.

Domain brief describes the deliverable and its later evidence. M02 implements only assigned production/demo/documentation surfaces; M03 authors independent assets, M04 checks, M08 qualifies and M09 alone performs admitted live attempts. Live-run verbs in this brief are not M02 dispatch authority.

### MI-NORMAL-005-BUILD - M02: Complete Atlas procedures and distributed lifecycle / NORMAL / BUILD

#### Purpose

Implement the complete coherent domain brief below, with one writer and shortest self-checks. Independent test author/reviewer own acceptance evidence. Return one reviewable tip; later repairs consume ROOT's full diagnosed objective and protected scope, not raw suggestions. Normal complete stage work and its independently selected evidence.

#### Coverage

DEL-005; REQ-005, REQ-006, REQ-007, REQ-008, REQ-009, REQ-010, REQ-013; STEP-005 NORMAL.

#### Selection basis

Required product implementation and repair tranche
 boundary; Normal complete stage work and its independently selected evidence.

#### Owner and roles

root owns bounded instance mechanics; root retains global decisions. Dispatch inventory: CARD-NORMAL-005-BUILD-EXEC=writer

#### Preconditions

STEP-003.accepted integrated output is verified; exact role resolution and scope authorization precede launch.

#### Inputs

STEP-003.accepted integrated output; REQ-005, REQ-006, REQ-007, REQ-008, REQ-009, REQ-010, REQ-013; current candidate and consumed state/checkpoints. Complete domain brief:

1. Implement immutable content plus separate approval, authorized recipients,
   current designation, publication partition, withdrawal and revocation state.
   A project-A approval never authorizes project B. Private content is local only.
   Curated procedure admission uses an explicit selection policy, never a whole
   ROOT skill-tree auto-approval.
2. Make remote approval evidence verifiable to the receiver through trusted
   configured writers/records. Origin-local paths, flags and hashes alone are
   insufficient. Preserve generated origin through approval and publication.
3. Implement causally ordered current designation and conditional writes. A late
   old publish cannot undo a newer designation/withdrawal; rollback is a new
   explicit designation to still-approved non-revoked content. Approval alone
   never supersedes another revision or partition.
4. Track all managed publications; source revocation blocks immediately and
   reconciles every partition plus in-flight writes before claiming completion.
   Distinguish one-partition withdrawal. Never reactivate old history implicitly.
   Record prior exposure for ROOT, without claiming already-delivered text erased.
5. Complete Vector Search index/prefilter coverage and exact payload/status/vector
   reads. Constructor paths must not auto-create indexes during retrieval. Verify
   real scoped vector and optional selected lexical behavior, publication lag,
   lost acknowledgement and stale retries. No hosted reranker is added implicitly.
6. Keep unchanged approved behavior separate from rebuilt projection fingerprints;
   full payload/metadata/query egress passes privacy and recipient checks.



Stage-local product gate (owned acceptance slice; executed by the later gate, not a command for the BUILD writer):

Procedure/Atlas assertions REQ-006/007/008/009 and procedure-owned REQ-005/010/013 clauses: immutable approved revision and designated pointer integrity; scoped real search with authoritative receiver validation; two publication partitions for global revocation versus narrower withdrawal; conditional write/lost acknowledgement recovery, stable-content reindexing and local fallback when optional Atlas is unavailable. Search/APC consumer clauses close STEP-006; integrated late-state closure STEP-007/009.

Domain brief describes the deliverable and its later evidence. M02 implements only assigned production/demo/documentation surfaces; M03 authors independent assets, M04 checks, M08 qualifies and M09 alone performs admitted live attempts. Live-run verbs in this brief are not M02 dispatch authority.

#### Outputs and results

MI-NORMAL-005-BUILD.reviewable product tip and smoke; native result and unique member output paths; factual reports are not acceptance.

#### Concurrency and isolation

Source frozen during collection. Separate native WORKTREE/member IDs and result/cache/temp roots; only disjoint adapter writes overlap. M04/M07 independent paths launch before waiting; Section10 shared budget, no added scheduler.

#### Resources and side effects

`P/src/memory_harness/procedures/`, `P/src/memory_harness/atlas/`,
`P/tests/{procedures,atlas}/`, scoped index/bootstrap definitions and operator
documentation. Any vendored MongoDB changes stay in its candidate copy and get
their own affected upstream tests; none is presumed necessary. Credentials remain protected configuration only. No live effects in M08; M09 exact allowed synthetic targets. Native resource IDs recorded/read back before cleanup; no broad deletion. Sessions unbounded, finite policy per P02.

#### Checks and acceptance

Return the assigned coherent reviewable production/demo/documentation tip, actual available smoke results and explicit unresolved facts. Independent M03/M04/M05 determine acceptance; self-report is not a product gate. Compile/import the changed source with its accepted environment and run the shortest already-available source-focused self-check. If independent M03 tests are not authored yet, report that fact; their later execution is not a prerequisite for returning this reviewable tip. Do not run downstream live campaigns or final safeguard. Stage product criteria remain the normal BUILD brief's explicit gate slice and root Section3; completion of this module is not automatic product acceptance.

#### Failure and exception routes

ROOT applies P07 owner routing from the complete pool: material product failure returns to the declared originating production M02 owner, strict test-only to that asset's M03 owner under P08, and operational/report fault stays with its factual producer under P09. The observing step does not gain write authority; P04 returns accepted repairs to the current progress bound, and P15 contains only observed live harm.

#### Prior results and change effects

P04 maps source/schema/config/runner/fixtures and actual external target state; keep historical results and unchanged PASS, rerun only earliest affected/unresolved set; no whole-plan diff invalidation.

#### Repeat, join, and terminal behavior

P02/P04/P07; complete pool, one join; ROOT decision precedes next module dispatch. No worker self-dispatch or unbounded replacements.

#### Cost and critical-path effect

2–4 engineering days for owning stage, not per instance. This M02 uses 1 terminal worker assignments only when active; local commands estimated5–120s, native scenario2–10min, service30–300s. Normal complete stage work and its independently selected evidence. Reuse eliminates only work with unchanged inputs, no measured savings claimed.

1. Ship regression repair, public interface change, dependency upgrade, schema/data

2. Implement typed cross-store normalization, scope/compatibility/approval gates,
   one-way and mutual conflict rejection, revision precedence, duplicate-storage
   neutrality, and independent skills/templates shortlist capacity.
3. Implement Standard and genuine Problem-focused (fallback to Standard without
   real failure context); bounded adaptive Deeper uses tested EverOS machinery,
   works locally when healthy, and admits bounded Atlas participation when enabled.
   Give each available participating store an attempt opportunity; preserve valid
   partial results and distinguish all participation statuses.
4. Enforce one primary decision per bounded objective, complete stage deadlines,
   service call/round/candidate limits, positive execution reserve, elapsed-time
   recalculation after restart, and unspecified-budget expensive-stage masking.
5. Use one sanitized canonical similarity representation across candidates.

   scores masquerading as APC similarity. Examine lower-ranked eligible candidates.
6. Preserve existing current-plan states; resolve only trusted current fields,
   enforce fixed steps and allowed edits, forbid inherited IDs/credit/permissions,
   and record attempted branch versus final ROOT-accepted plan source. No direct
   API or ROOT self-rewrite substitutes for light adaptation.
7. Keep project-topology admission separate. Late Level 0 invalidates its old
   preparation; at most the configured bounded reprepare or explicit no-memory
   continuation retains exact lineage and spent usage. It never relabels stale data.

2–4 engineering days for owning stage, not per instance. This M02 uses 1 terminal worker assignments only when active; local commands estimated5–120s, native scenario2–10min, service30–300s. #### Outputs and results

MI-FL2-S1-006-PATCH.reviewable product tip and smoke; native result and unique member output paths; factual reports are not acceptance.

#### Concurrency and isolation

Source frozen during collection. Separate native WORKTREE/member IDs and result/cache/temp roots; one writer at shared seams. M04/M07 independent paths launch before waiting; Section10 shared budget, no added scheduler.

#### Resources and side effects

`P/src/memory_harness/{search,templates,apc}/`, five reviewed template
assets, `P/tests/{search,templates,apc}/`; candidate harness delegation adapter
only through ROOT-owned seam changes. Credentials remain protected configuration only. No live effects in M08; M09 exact allowed synthetic targets. Native resource IDs recorded/read back before cleanup; no broad deletion. Sessions unbounded, finite policy per P02.

#### Checks and acceptance

Return the assigned coherent reviewable production/demo/documentation tip, actual available smoke results and explicit unresolved facts. Independent M03/M04/M05 determine acceptance; self-report is not a product gate. Run changed-source compile and ROOT-named exact motivating test only, retaining its smoke receipt; no broad campaign. Stage product criteria remain the normal BUILD brief's explicit gate slice and root Section3; completion of this module is not automatic product acceptance.

#### Failure and exception routes

ROOT applies P07 owner routing from the complete pool: material product failure returns to the declared originating production M02 owner, strict test-only to that asset's M03 owner under P08, and operational/report fault stays with its factual producer under P09. The observing step does not gain write authority; P04 returns accepted repairs to the current progress bound, and P15 contains only observed live harm.

#### Prior results and change effects

P04 maps source/schema/config/runner/fixtures and actual external target state; keep historical results and unchanged PASS, rerun only earliest affected/unresolved set; no whole-plan diff invalidation.

#### Repeat, join, and terminal behavior

After acceptance and separate integration, exit only to ROOT's selected current progress bound through its Series 2 entry. P02/P04/P07; complete pool, one join; accepted integrated repaired output exits to current-bound Series 2 only. No worker self-dispatch or unbounded replacements.

#### Cost and critical-path effect

2–4 engineering days for owning stage, not per instance. This M02 uses 1 terminal worker assignments only when active; local commands estimated5–120s, native scenario2–10min, service30–300s. Outbound scoped repair only. Required smoke is changed-source compile plus the exact motivating tests from ROOT's diagnosed correction card. No broad deterministic campaign; independent review and separate accepted integration retained. Reuse eliminates only work with unchanged inputs, no measured savings claimed.

### MI-NORMAL-007-BUILD - M02: Complete context, composition, provider and switch boundaries / NORMAL / BUILD

#### Purpose

Implement the complete coherent domain brief below, with one writer and shortest self-checks. Independent test author/reviewer own acceptance evidence. Return one reviewable tip; later repairs consume ROOT's full diagnosed objective and protected scope, not raw suggestions. Normal complete stage work and its independently selected evidence.

#### Coverage

DEL-007; REQ-001, REQ-003, REQ-006, REQ-011, REQ-016, REQ-017, REQ-018, REQ-021, REQ-024; STEP-007 NORMAL.

#### Selection basis

Required product implementation and repair tranche
 boundary; Normal complete stage work and its independently selected evidence.

#### Owner and roles

root owns bounded instance mechanics; root retains global decisions. Dispatch inventory: CARD-NORMAL-007-BUILD-EXEC=complex_writer

#### Preconditions

STEP-006.accepted integrated output is verified; exact role resolution and scope authorization precede launch.

#### Inputs

STEP-006.accepted integrated output; REQ-001, REQ-003, REQ-006, REQ-011, REQ-016, REQ-017, REQ-018, REQ-021, REQ-024; current candidate and consumed state/checkpoints. Complete domain brief:

1. Compose the ROOT suite and harness by explicit ownership, stage/validate before
   commit, preserve repository instructions/shared provider configuration, and
   retain recovery evidence for partial installation. Setup twice is idempotent;
   later upstream overwrite of staged payloads is detected before enhanced dispatch.
2. Pack labeled current truth, historical evidence, coherent approved skills and
   exact accepted plan with output contract. Never execute a draft as authority or
   truncate mandatory state to fit optional memory. Account for full usable window,
   history and output reserve; oversized mandatory context uses ROOT recovery.
3. Bind actual rendered payload, accepted plan/review, task/objective/decision,
   repository/base and delivered items to bootstrap/resume validation. Distinguish
   selected, packed, dispatched and ambiguous launch acknowledgement. Reconcile
   before repeat launch; omit optional guidance only with corrected trace/envelope.
4. Recheck live shared eligibility at selection/use and final delivery after review
   under its own reserve/budget. A revoked or uncheckable plan dependency returns
   to ROOT; cached shared bytes cannot be relabeled local. Existing accepted plans
   remain exact state; known unsafe exposure goes to ROOT.
5. Complete independent feature toggles and actual provider/service suppression,
   dependency resolution and pending/in-flight off transitions. All-off emits no
   optional calls, implicit curated retrieval or memory/APC context; local exact
   evidence remains durable. Explicit safety operations remain separately authorized.
6. Scrub control credentials from every ordinary worker arm and all transport
   surfaces; validate any task-only protected credential path or block it. Implement
   Codex/Claude actual staged tool controls for declared network modes and record
   residual shell egress. No unsupported hard-isolation claims.



Stage-local product gate (owned acceptance slice; executed by the later gate, not a command for the BUILD writer):

Composition/context/security assertions REQ-001/002/003/011/016/017/018/021/024 and consumed REQ-006 clauses: preserved pristine export versus composed union, exact accepted envelope at bootstrap/resume, actual staged/loaded data and launched tools for both advertised providers, late revocation, all ten switches and meaningful dependencies/transitions/all-off, no unauthorized calls or leaked fixture canaries. Strong egress claim is accepted only with independently enforced boundary; absence is honest unsupported evidence, never a mock PASS. Integrated closure is STEP-009.

Domain brief describes the deliverable and its later evidence. M02 implements only assigned production/demo/documentation surfaces; M03 authors independent assets, M04 checks, M08 qualifies and M09 alone performs admitted live attempts. Live-run verbs in this brief are not M02 dispatch authority.

#### Inputs

STEP-007.accepted integrated output; REQ-019, REQ-020, REQ-022, REQ-023; current candidate and consumed state/checkpoints. Complete domain brief:

1. Recover prepared, finalized, dispatch-pending, confirmed, abandoned and outcome
   facts by exact identity. Contradictory terminal records conflict; identical
   replay is idempotent; explicit upstream correction retains linked history.
   Missing review remains unobserved, distinct from genuine terminal unrecognized.
2. Reconcile every enabled side effect from durable intent/evidence after crashes.
   Preserve uncertainty where underlying effects cannot be queried safely.
   Conditional local concurrency cannot lose/overwrite accepted work; disabled
   non-safety operations pause while authorized revocation remains possible.
3. Normalize native usage by invocation across corrections/resumes, including ROOT,
   planning/review, APC and worker calls; prevent cumulative/cache/reasoning double
   counts. Keep outer testing overhead separate, capture expected calls/missing
   receipts, abandoned work and late maintenance without changing quality outcomes.
4. Implement logical snapshot/export/restore with quiesced owned writers or proven
   consistency. Include approvals, current/tombstone state, receipts/provenance,
   representation/configuration and explicit pending effects. Namespace-bound
   restoration is the V1 choice; no speculative cross-namespace remapper. Verify
   completeness and hashes before accepting restored capabilities.
5. Expose actionable setup/readiness, prepare/finalize, procedure lifecycle, pending
   inspection/reconciliation, template repair, outcome/usage and snapshot operations.
   Test normal use, refusal and recovery without secret output. Retention preserves
   audit/reproducibility; no automatic procedure/outcome history deletion.

#### Outputs and results

MI-NORMAL-008-BUILD.reviewable product tip and smoke; native result and unique member output paths; factual reports are not acceptance.

#### Concurrency and isolation

Source frozen during collection. Separate native WORKTREE/member IDs and result/cache/temp roots; one writer at shared seams. M04/M07 independent paths launch before waiting; Section10 shared budget, no added scheduler.

#### Resources and side effects

`P/src/memory_harness/{evidence,reconcile,usage,snapshots,cli}/`,
`P/tests/{recovery,usage,snapshots,cli}/`, product operator docs. Serial ownership
of evidence code previously established by STEP-002. Credentials remain protected configuration only. No live effects in M08; M09 exact allowed synthetic targets. Native resource IDs recorded/read back before cleanup; no broad deletion. Sessions unbounded, finite policy per P02.

#### Checks and acceptance

Return the assigned coherent reviewable production/demo/documentation tip, actual available smoke results and explicit unresolved facts. Independent M03/M04/M05 determine acceptance; self-report is not a product gate. Compile/import the changed source with its accepted environment and run the shortest already-available source-focused self-check. If independent M03 tests are not authored yet, report that fact; their later execution is not a prerequisite for returning this reviewable tip. Do not run downstream live campaigns or final safeguard. Stage product criteria remain the normal BUILD brief's explicit gate slice and root Section3; completion of this module is not automatic product acceptance.

#### Failure and exception routes

ROOT applies P07 owner routing from the complete pool: material product failure returns to the declared originating production M02 owner, strict test-only to that asset's M03 owner under P08, and operational/report fault stays with its factual producer under P09. The observing step does not gain write authority; P04 returns accepted repairs to the current progress bound, and P15 contains only observed live harm.

#### Prior results and change effects

P04 maps source/schema/config/runner/fixtures and actual external target state; keep historical results and unchanged PASS, rerun only earliest affected/unresolved set; no whole-plan diff invalidation.

#### Repeat, join, and terminal behavior

P02/P04/P07; complete pool, one join; ROOT decision precedes next module dispatch. No worker self-dispatch or unbounded replacements.

#### Cost and critical-path effect

2–4 engineering days for owning stage, not per instance. This M02 uses 1 terminal worker assignments only when active; local commands estimated5–120s, native scenario2–10min, service30–300s. Normal complete stage work and its independently selected evidence. Reuse eliminates only work with unchanged inputs, no measured savings claimed.

MI-FL2-S1-008-PATCH.reviewable product tip and smoke; native result and unique member output paths; factual reports are not acceptance.

#### Concurrency and isolation

Source frozen during collection. Separate native WORKTREE/member IDs and result/cache/temp roots; one writer at shared seams. M04/M07 independent paths launch before waiting; Section10 shared budget, no added scheduler.

#### Resources and side effects

`P/src/memory_harness/{evidence,reconcile,usage,snapshots,cli}/`,
`P/tests/{recovery,usage,snapshots,cli}/`, product operator docs. Serial ownership
of evidence code previously established by STEP-002. Credentials remain protected configuration only. No live effects in M08; M09 exact allowed synthetic targets. Native resource IDs recorded/read back before cleanup; no broad deletion. Sessions unbounded, finite policy per P02.

#### Checks and acceptance

Return the assigned coherent reviewable production/demo/documentation tip, actual available smoke results and explicit unresolved facts. Independent M03/M04/M05 determine acceptance; self-report is not a product gate. Run changed-source compile and ROOT-named exact motivating test only, retaining its smoke receipt; no broad campaign. Stage product criteria remain the normal BUILD brief's explicit gate slice and root Section3; completion of this module is not automatic product acceptance.

#### Failure and exception routes

ROOT applies P07 owner routing from the complete pool: material product failure returns to the declared originating production M02 owner, strict test-only to that asset's M03 owner under P08, and operational/report fault stays with its factual producer under P09. The observing step does not gain write authority; P04 returns accepted repairs to the current progress bound, and P15 contains only observed live harm.

#### Prior results and change effects

P04 maps source/schema/config/runner/fixtures and actual external target state; keep historical results and unchanged PASS, rerun only earliest affected/unresolved set; no whole-plan diff invalidation.

#### Repeat, join, and terminal behavior

After acceptance and separate integration, exit only to ROOT's selected current progress bound through its Series 2 entry. P02/P04/P07; complete pool, one join; accepted integrated repaired output exits to current-bound Series 2 only. No worker self-dispatch or unbounded replacements.

#### Cost and critical-path effect

2–4 engineering days for owning stage, not per instance. This M02 uses 1 terminal worker assignments only when active; local commands estimated5–120s, native scenario2–10min, service30–300s. Outbound scoped repair only. Required smoke is changed-source compile plus the exact motivating tests from ROOT's diagnosed correction card. No broad deterministic campaign; independent review and separate accepted integration retained. Reuse eliminates only work with unchanged inputs, no measured savings claimed.

### MI-NORMAL-009-BUILD - M02: Integrated candidate verification and synthetic demonstration / NORMAL / BUILD

#### Purpose

Implement the complete coherent domain brief below, with one writer and shortest self-checks. Independent test author/reviewer own acceptance evidence. Return one reviewable tip; later repairs consume ROOT's full diagnosed objective and protected scope, not raw suggestions. Normal complete stage work and its independently selected evidence.

#### Coverage

DEL-009; REQ-020, REQ-022, REQ-024, REQ-025, REQ-026, REQ-027, REQ-028, REQ-029, REQ-030; STEP-009 NORMAL.

#### Selection basis

Required product implementation and repair tranche
 boundary; Normal complete stage work and its independently selected evidence.

#### Owner and roles

root owns bounded instance mechanics; root retains global decisions. Dispatch inventory: CARD-NORMAL-009-BUILD-EXEC=writer

#### Preconditions

STEP-008.accepted integrated output is verified; exact role resolution and scope authorization precede launch.

#### Inputs

STEP-008.accepted integrated output; REQ-020, REQ-022, REQ-024, REQ-025, REQ-026, REQ-027, REQ-028, REQ-029, REQ-030; current candidate and consumed state/checkpoints. Complete domain brief:

1. Run the reviewed assertion matrix with independent coordinates concurrent under
   their actual resources. Exercise routine role, chosen middleweight, heavyweight role, both advertised
   providers' applicable behavior and the explicitly identical APC A/B profiles, preserving separate configuration-contract proof without
   crossing every scenario with every model/backend.
2. Verify synthetic baseline and enhanced task paths, both normal and refusal/
   recovery cases, real EverOS generation and Atlas Vector Search, current-plan
   precedence, all APC branches, review linkage and durable cross-session behavior.
3. Exercise child timeout/cleanup, lost dispatch acknowledgement, test-ROOT failure,
   snapshot restore, process/lease collision, late revocation and off transitions.
   Confirm candidate source/entrypoints, actual model bindings and outer/inner
   evidence/resource separation. Exact linked artifacts decide results.
4. Demonstrate reviewed experience from task A affecting a related new session B,
   a visible Atlas discovery, accepted reuse/guidance and verified outcome, plus a
   deliberately incompatible procedure rejected into normal planning. Use original
   purpose-built disposable tasks, never benchmark tasks or scored comparisons.
5. Collect all feasible results before repairs; group causes and re-run only
   invalidated evidence after fixes. Unavailable required services/models/host proof
   remain blocked coverage, never PASS. Verify no benchmark/learner task is in the
   test workload or queued after closeout.



Stage-local product gate (owned acceptance slice; executed by the later gate, not a command for the BUILD writer):

All current T01–T29 (REQ-001–029) and integrated REQ-030 are established by actual candidate-bound local/service/native evidence, including cross-session synthetic demonstration, successful actual A/B execution of the identical selected tuple plus independently established two-choice configuration-contract selection, actual nested ownership/timeout/test-ROOT failure cleanup, independent adjudicated review, usage completeness and no unproven owned cleanup. Reuse only unchanged inputs; local unit or outer-harness success cannot substitute for required native/service observations.

Domain brief describes the deliverable and its later evidence. M02 implements only assigned production/demo/documentation surfaces; M03 authors independent assets, M04 checks, M08 qualifies and M09 alone performs admitted live attempts. Live-run verbs in this brief are not M02 dispatch authority.
